# AI Coder Core Test Error Log

`npm test` appends a timestamped result for every invocation here, including source fingerprint, commit, test counts and failure traces. Detailed per-test records are retained in `.galaxy/tests/<run>/events.jsonl`, `summary.json` and `summary.md`.

The Node reporter is development tooling owned by this repository. It does not import galaxy-code, add a runtime dependency or change published exports. Audits launched from galaxy-code record their core test results in that audit's journal instead.

For cross-repository live incidents, see [Galaxy investigation](../../galaxy-code/docs/TEST_FAILURE_ANALYSIS.md) when working in the Galaxy workspace. This link is contextual; the standalone core tests do not require that repository.
